- **nielsen-heuristics**: Run a formal heuristic evaluation of a design artifact against Jakob Nielsen's 10 usability heuristics, producing an evidence-backed, severity-scored report with honesty guards. Renders as markdown or a Tufte-style HTML report, and can export findings as Linear or Beads tasks after confirmation. Triggers on heuristic evaluation, usability review, Nielsen heuristics check, UX heuristic audit, usability inspection.
